## Document 020: 2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b

Source PDF: all report pdf/2026-10-14_［みずほ経済EXPRESS］日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ_5c83f50b.pdf
Report source: Mizuho
Date: 2026-10-14
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1

みずほ経済 EXPRESS

日本の原油輸入価格は今後さらに上昇へ 
～物価上振れリスクが強まれば、一段の利上げ加速も～

調査部 主任エコノミスト 東深澤 武史 
池田 亮平 
エコノミスト 阿部 大樹 
シニア日本経済エコノミスト 越山 祐資 
080-1069-4919  takeshi.higashifukasawa@mizuho-rt.co.jp 
■ 日銀は物価上振れ抑制に舵。中東情勢に起因する物価押し上げリスクは予断を許さず 
9 月日銀会合後の会見で植田総裁は「政策局面が変化した」と述べた（図表 1） 。これまで日銀は、
基調的物価の押し上げを金融政策運営の重点としてきたが、足元では 2％前後の物価上昇が定着しつ
つあるとみられる。こうしたもとで、金融政策運営の重点は物価上振れの回避に移っており、そのた
めに緩和的な金融環境をなるべく早く脱することが重視される。日銀の基本的なスタンスが、連続利
上げや、0.25％の利上げ幅を上回る大幅利上げは回避するのが望ましいというものであることも踏ま
えると、今後の利上げペースは 3 カ月に一回程度、0.25％ずつが基本になるだろう 1。 
ただし、金融政策の重点が物価上振れ回避にシフトしているなかでは、よりフォワードルッキング
な政策運営が求められる 2。したがって、 原油調達コストの上昇や円安など、 物価上振れリスクの強ま
り次第で利上げペースが速まる可能性がある。先行きの物価を押し上げる主なリスク要因として、日
図表1 9月会合後の総裁会見のポイント 図表2 WTI原油先物

（出所） 日本銀行より、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成 （出所） LSEGより、みずほ総合研究所調査部作成

### Page 2

2

銀は、原油高等の中東情勢影響、AI 関連需要の強さ、円安の三点を挙げている。このうち、原油価格
は足元で再び上昇しており、先行きの不確実性も大きい。中東情勢は、物価を取り巻く現下最大のリ
スクだろう。 
■ 不安定な情勢下で再び原油価格が上昇。タンカー運賃も急騰しダブルパンチの様相 
WTI原油先物は、9月に入って一時1バレル=100ドル台と、5月中旬以来の水準に上昇した （図表2） 。
足元では原油価格は幾分低下しているものの、水準としては依然高い状態が続いている。背景には、
中東産原油の供給懸念が強まったことがある。7月下旬以降、 サウジアラビアとイエメン・フーシ派に
よる戦闘が開始し、 ホルムズ海峡を代替する重要な原油輸送ルートとして活用されてきたバブ ・ エル ・
マンデブ海峡の通航リスクが高まった。 また、9月に入り、 サウジアラビアの原油パイプラインが攻撃
を受けて停止したと伝わると、中東産原油の域外輸送が一段と困難になるとの見方が広がった。 
中東情勢が再度悪化するなかで上昇しているのは原油価格だけではない。原油価格の上昇を大幅に
上回る勢いで、原油タンカーの運賃が急騰している（図表3）。8月以降に上昇基調が鮮明となり、9月
中旬には一時、中東情勢悪化前（2026年初）の約46倍、情勢悪化後の3～7月頃と比べても約3.2倍に達
した。 海峡航行リスクが高まるもと、 積み替えや迂回によって原油輸送の効率が悪化しているためだ。
海事データを活用して商品市場を分析しているベルギーの調査会社Kplerによると、 ホルムズ海峡の内
側にあるペルシャ湾から原油を運び出す船と、海峡の外側から最終目的地まで運ぶ船を分け、洋上で
原油を積み替える方式が広がっているようだ。 長距離輸送を担う船が危険な航路を通らずに済む反面、
積み替え作業や待機に時間を要するため、船を次の輸送に回しにくくなる。また、危険な海域を避け
るために遠回りする輸送も増え、船が一度の航海に費やす日数も長くなっている。より多くの輸送資
源が必要となったことで船舶需給がひっ迫し、タンカー運賃が大きく押し上げられている格好だ。

図表3 原油タンカーの運賃 図表4 原油輸入単価と原油価格

ン）のTCE。TCEは、運賃収入から燃料費や港湾費等を控除した1日
当たり換算収益。日本を含む東アジア向け運賃の代理指標。

のドバイ原油価格＝2 月のドバイ原油価格×3 月のWTI原油先物の
前月比の伸びとして試算。中東情勢悪化を背景に、3月はドバイ原油
の価格形成機能が損なわれたと指摘されているため。原油輸入単価

### Page 3

■ 今後は日本の原油調達コストが上昇。企業間取引価格は反転加速を予想 
原油価格の上昇に原油タンカー運賃の急騰が重なり、先行きの日本の原油輸入単価（単位量あたり
の原油調達コスト）は一段と上昇する見込みだ3。 中東産原油が日本に輸送されるまでには1～2カ月程
度の期間を要するため、8月中旬以降の原油価格上昇 ・原油タンカー運賃上昇は10月以降の原油輸入単
価を押し上げ、その後国内の企業間取引価格へと影響が波及していくと考えられる。足元の原油価格
とタンカー運賃を踏まえると、10月の原油輸入単価は1バレル＝約140ドル程度と、中東情勢悪化後で
最も高い水準に上昇する可能性が高い（図表4）。この影響を輸入全体の平均価格（円建てベース）で
みると、10月の伸びは前年比＋29％程度と、中東情勢悪化後に最も輸入物価の伸びが加速した6・7月
に迫る勢いになる計算だ。さらに、その後は、石油化学製品などの関連財を中心に、国内の企業間取
引価格が上昇しよう。 足元で上昇圧力が一服しつつある国内企業物価は再び加速し、11月頃に同＋7％
台の伸びになると予想する（図表5）。 
加えて、原油輸入金額が膨らんで貿易収支が悪化すれば、円安圧力が強まる可能性がある。原油の
代替調達が進展した6月以降は1兆円に迫る貿易赤字が続いているが、特に10月以降は原油輸入単価が
上昇するもとで本邦貿易赤字が拡大する公算が大きく、実需面からの円安圧力が強まりやすい（図表
6） 。 足元の為替相場は、 米国の利上げ観測を背景に円安に振れやすい地合いだ。 そこに実需の動きが
重なり、1ドル＝160円を上回るようなドル高円安が進行すれば、輸入物価の上振れを通じて、国内企
業物価の伸びが一層高まるリスクがある。 
■ 物価上昇圧力が長期化する場合は利上げペース加速も視野に 
以上のように、当面の企業間取引価格に対しては押し上げ圧力が持続する公算が大きい。物価上昇
圧力が長期化するほど消費者物価への価格転嫁姿勢も強まる可能性があるだけに、日銀は物価上振れ
に対する高い警戒レベルを維持することになろう。 
仮に物価上振れリスクが大きく高まる場合には、 連続利上げ ・大幅利上げの可能性も排除できない。
中東情勢の混乱が長期化し、27年以降も原油輸入単価が一段と上振れることが見込まれる場合、ある
図表5 輸入物価（円建て）と国内企業物価 図表6 貿易収支

### Page 4

4

いは1ドル=160円を持続的に上回る水準まで円安が進展する場合には 、連続利上げや大幅利上げのシ
ナリオが現実味を帯びてくるだろう。

1 中立金利の水準は不透明であり、利上げの影響はタイムラグをともなって現れる。連続利上げ・大幅利上げによって意図せず
金融引き締めに転じれば、経済へのマイナス影響が必要以上に強まるリスクがある。 
2 中東情勢悪化以降で最初の利上げを決定した 6 月会合を振り返っても、日銀は川上の企業物価の加速を理由の一つとして利上
げに踏み切っている。6 月会合の声明文において日銀は、国内企業物価上昇率が前年比＋6.3％（5 月時点の速報ベース）に加速
したことを踏まえ、 「企業間取引におけるやや速いスピードでの価格転嫁が、消費者段階における幅広い品目の価格上昇に波及
していく可能性がある」と評価した。 
3 中東情勢悪化以降、日本の原油輸入単価は国際市況から大きく上振れている。背景として、主な代替調達先である米国産原油
が需給ひっ迫の懸念等から値上がりし、割高になったことなどが挙げられる。経済産業省発表によると、秋口以降は中東産原油
の調達が増加し、割高な米国産原油に対する依存度は低下すると見込まれるものの、本文記載のように中東産原油の輸送コスト
が大幅に高まっていることから、先行きも割高感の解消は見込みづらい。

市場焦點
美國
科技股
• 美國企業獲利維持強勁 ，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及 AI資本支出熱潮支撐 。從獲利來看 ，市
場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，
雖呈現逐步降溫 ，但仍維持雙位數增長 。標普500預期本益比已由約 23倍降至19倍，且並非
主要來自股價修正 ，而是企業獲利預期上調所致 ，在企業獲利維持成長 、AI投資持續挹注及
估值已較高點收斂的情況下，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。
美債
外匯對沖視角
• 在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總
回報優於多數G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率落底訊號以重返市場 ，
對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言 ，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位 ，具備

相當良好的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
台股
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈，綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡
的紅燈，連續第9個月亮出紅燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。
• 根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減 ，年底集團作帳行情以
及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表
現，有望上看53,000點。此外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。

就業增幅明顯放緩，降低Fed短期再升息急迫性
• 非農明顯降溫，勞動市場尚未出現全面惡化：9月非農僅增2.9萬人，遠低於預期，且7、8月合計下修6萬人；失業率升
至4.2%，但勞參率同步升至 61.8%，家戶調查就業增加 40.6萬人，顯示失業率上升部分來自勞動供給增加 ，其中營建、
製造業仍分別增加1.1萬及0.9萬人，平均工時維持34.4小時，數據呈現招聘動能明顯放慢，但尚未出現全面性就業惡化。
• 薪資增速放緩，Fed短期政策壓力下降：9月平均時薪月增僅0.1%、年增3.0%，均低於前值；同時8月PCE年增3.4%、
核心PCE年增3.0%，顯示薪資通膨壓力有所緩和 ，但整體物價仍高於Fed 2%目標，在9月Fed升息1碼後，非農報告降
低Fed在10月連續升息的必要性，市場焦點轉向年底通膨與AI投資驅動的產業動能。

• 投資建議：短期關注美國中短天期投資級債券 ，就業降溫與薪資放緩有助降低短端利率上行壓力 ，但通膨仍高於目標 ，
因此不宜過度 預期長天期利率快速回落；股市則可續關注 AI基礎建設與資本支出相關產業 ，因營建及製造業就業仍具
韌性，顯示企業投資動能尚未明顯轉弱。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics， 2026.10.3

消費動能回升、通膨低於預期，內需韌性與物價壓力並存
• PCE低於預期、通膨降溫仍待確認：美國8月PCE物價指數月增0.3%、年增3.4%，核心PCE月增0.2%、年增3.0%，均低於
市場預期；其中核心PCE較修訂後7月的3.3%下降，顯示物價壓力有所緩解，但仍高於Fed 2%目標，此次年度更新回溯修
訂2021年以來資料，亦使歷史基期重新調整，後續仍須觀察通膨下降是否能延續。
• 消費回升、所得增幅偏緩：8月個人所得月增0.2%，個人消費支出（PCE）月增0.9%、實質消費成長0.6%，顯示內需仍具
韌性；惟消費增幅明顯高於所得 ，個人儲蓄率由7月4.6%降至4.1%，為2022年11月以來低點，反映家庭可支應消費的財
務緩衝持續縮小，後續消費動能仍須觀察。
• 後市觀察：PCE低於預期後，市場對10月再升息的預期明顯降溫，但長端美債殖利率仍受通膨、財政供給與期限溢價牽動

而維持高檔；建議可聚焦獲利成長與現金流穩健的優質大型成長股與短天期美債以掌握收益 ，同時留意儲蓄率下滑與 9月
消費者信心低迷可能抑制後續消費動能，動態調整部位並嚴控風險。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Economic Analysis， 2026.10.1

能源與AI推升長端利率，融資成本壓力延續
• 長端殖利率上行 、期限溢價成重要推力 ：近期
美債呈現短端與長端走勢分化 ，雖市場對 10月
Fed再升息預期因 8月PCE低於預期而明顯降溫 ，
10年期及30年期的美債殖利率仍偏高位 ，反映
通膨風險 、財政融資需求與期限溢價上升 ，長
天期無風險利率維持高檔 ，並推升房貸及企業
融資成本。
• 能源與AI投資推升通膨及資金需求 ：油價維持
高檔增加通膨黏著風險 ，AI基礎建設投資則帶
動企業資本支出與融資需求 ，兩者共同提高長
期資金需求；在財政赤字與美債供給仍大的環
境下，長端殖利率可能維持較高水準 ，進而提
高股票估值折現率及高槓桿企業的融資壓力。
• 後市觀察： 短期持續觀察油價 、10年及30年期
美債殖利率與期限溢價變化；債券配置仍建議

降低長天期債券部位以降低利率風險 ，優先布
局短天期高品質債券獲取穩健息收 ，股票則聚
焦具穩健現金流 、低財務槓桿與定價能力的企
業，以降低高利率環境下的估值與融資風險。

AI需求大增，美光財測超過市場預期
• AI記憶體需求強勁，美光財測顯示景氣延續：美光科技9/30盤後公布財報，2026財年的第四季營收達542.3億美元，年增

379%，調整後EPS 33.42美元，均優於市場預期；公司並預估2027會計年度第一季營收615億美元、調整後EPS 38.15美元，
分別高於市場預期的567.7億美元及36.02美元。強勁財測反映AI資料中心持續推升HBM、伺服器DRAM及高速SSD需求。
隨Amazon、Alphabet等大型科技企業持續擴大AI基礎建設投資，記憶體需求仍具成長動能。
• 長約與產品升級，支撐中長期業績：美光目前已簽署36份多年期供應協議，涵蓋約三分之一營收並延伸至2030年，有助降
低記憶體產業週期波動；同時持續布局HBM4、HBM4E及高容量DDR5等產品。公司2026會計年度自由現金流達590億美元，
並預期2027、2028會計年度供需將進一步緊俏。整體而言，AI需求已由單一HBM擴展至伺服器記憶體與儲存產品，美光財
測亦顯示景氣延續性仍強，後續市場焦點將轉向擴產速度、資本支出及AI需求成長能否維持。
資料來源：美光，彭博，鉅亨網，2026.10.1

• AI驅動獲利成長，企業基本面仍具支撐：美國企業獲利維持強勁，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及AI資本支出熱潮支撐。從獲利
來看，市場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，雖呈現逐步降溫，但
仍維持雙位數增長。其中，AI基礎建設與大型雲端業者仍是重要驅動來源，2026年對EPS成長的貢獻合計約19%，2027、
2028年仍分別貢獻14%及11%。
• 獲利上修降低估值壓力：標普500預期本益比已由約23倍降至19倍，且並非主要來自股價修正，而是企業獲利預期上調所致。
雖然未來EPS成長率若由高檔回落，市場仍可能面臨評價修正壓力，但在企業獲利維持成長、AI投資持續挹注及估值已較高
點收斂的情況下，標普500基本面仍具支撐。因此，短期應留意獲利增速放緩與市場持倉偏高所帶來的波動，但從獲利成長

與估值兩大面向觀察，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。

2026年製造業PMI 2024年 2025年 2023年
生產回升與價格上漲同步推動中國PMI改善
• 生產回升與價格上漲同步推動 中國PMI改善： 9月製造業PMI升至50.1%，較前月回升0.3個百分點，重返榮枯線以上，顯示
製造業景氣有所改善 ，主要受生產活動回暖及價格上漲帶動 ，中小企業景氣同步改善 ，但仍低於榮枯線，大型企業表現相對
較佳，高技術製造、裝備製造維持擴張 ，消費品PMI升至50.7%，重回景氣區間 ，另一方面，原材料及能源價格上漲推升企
業成本，上下游獲利差距持續擴大 。 非製造業PMI則由49.0%升至50.2%，服務業受節慶效應帶動回溫 ，建築業則受財政資

金投入，及基建建設推進而改善，不過需求復甦仍偏溫和，企業成本壓力尚未明顯減輕。
• 結論：第四季隨著基建投資、財政支出及房市支持政策逐步落地，經濟可望獲得支撐，但10月受長假因素影響，PMI可能短
期承壓。

台灣景氣燈號連9紅 台股Q4有望突破5萬點大關
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈： 綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡的紅燈 ，連續第9個月亮出紅
燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。國發會表示，AI需求令台廠積極擴廠，加上政府推動AI新十大建
設，民間投資有望維持動能。此外，受惠財富效應、企業調薪及就業市場穩定，民間消費有望持續成長。
• 法人看好台股年底前站上5萬點：根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減，年底集團作帳行情
以及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表現 ，有望上看53,000點。此
外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。
• 觀點：近期台股站上48,000點整數關卡，總市值達5.3兆美元，讓台股耀升成為全球第四大市值股市 ，顯示台股在AI浪潮下

的得天獨厚。年底前仍須留意中東衝突進展，若能源通膨壓力持續，聯準會升息預期恐為股市帶來波動，但考慮AI科技持續
發酵，建議投資人續抱台股，並以定期不定額的策略紀律加碼，目標追求中長期完整的AI趨勢回報。
資料來源：國發會，2026.09.29。

外匯對沖視角—美債對沖後殖利率屬全球最低之一
• 美國國債殖利率真的高嗎？在當前全球總體經濟環境下，美國國債殖利率雖維持在數十年歷史高點附近，但若單憑名目殖利
率來判斷美債的吸引力，容易忽視匯率對沖與各國財政風險帶來的溢價差異。
• 美元視角下的殖利率比較： 若將其他已開發市場的主權債務 (如德債、日債、英債)完全對沖回美元，美債在殖利率曲線上多
數期限的實際殖利率，其實與德債並列全球最低水準。相反地，日本國債與英國國債經匯率對沖回美元後的收益率反而更高。
• 跨貨幣資產交換 (ASW)的真實回報： 以10年期主權債的對沖跨貨幣資產交換計算：美國 10年期國債有效殖利率約 4.85%，
日本10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約5.06%，德國10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約4.51%，這表明美國政府發

債的真實對沖融資成本其實已相對精實，且符合國際市場對各國財政基本面的合理定價。

財政基本面與信用風險—殖利率溢價反映國家債務比重
• 從歐元計價或信用風險的角度來比較 ，美債的定價邏輯非常符合總體經濟基本面： 儘管債務以歐元計價時 ，10年期美債殖
利率比同期限德國國債高出約30個基點，這反映出德國的債務佔GDP比重僅約65%，而美國則高達127%，因此美債承擔了
適度的財政溢價。儘管義大利的債務佔GDP比重高達140%且信用前景較遜色，但在統一貨幣基準下，義大利國債殖利率仍
較美國國債高出60個基點，這顯示市場並未過度壓低美債信用，美債依然享有優質避險資產的溢價優勢。
• 海外私人投資者的資金流向：近一年海外私人機構在美債加碼速度放緩，乃通膨環境下全球其他資產選擇增加，及過去升息
波段導致帳面按市值計價虧損 。然而歐洲投資人(持有一半以上的海外美債)目前仍持有龐大部位，並在等待適當的加碼時機。

• 小結與投資建議：在經過外匯對沖後，美債收益率在發達國家主權債中處於相對低位，發行成本合理且具競爭力。美債殖利
率高於德債低於義債 ，精確地反映美國政府債務負擔率 (127%)與總體經濟穩健度 。在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，
美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總回報優於多數 G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率
落底訊號以重返市場，對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位，具備相當良好
的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。

亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健
• 亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健：儘管AI熱潮推動收⼊創下紀錄 ，但SK海力⼠和台積電的美元債 ，今年以來的表現仍
略遜於彭博亞洲美元信用債總回報指數 ，主因為大規模資本支出擴張 ，以及潛在的投⼊資本回報率下降令 其利差承壓，美國
雲服務巨頭創紀錄的 AI支出計畫加劇了壓力 ，儘管如此 SK 海力⼠和台積電仍保持較強的高評級信用實力 ，台積電計畫投
⼊600-640億美元資本支出 ，SK海力⼠的資本支出則處於 40兆韓圜區間。兩家公司穩健的資產負債表 、合計共1,260億美元
的淨現金、強勁的經營性現金流，以及在先進製程，和HBM晶⽚領域的市場領先地位，為上述支出提供支撐。
• 投資建議：現階段台積電與SK海力⼠美元債最大的問題不是信用風險 ，而是估值與利差壓力 ，短期可能因AI資本支出過大，

及市場情緒波動而表現落後指數 ，但以兩家公司強大的現金流 、淨現金部位及產業領導地位來看 ，信用品質仍屬亞洲投資級
債中的優質標的，建議利差擴大時分批布局 。

顧 • 企業獲利成長將支撐股價上行：標普500指數的長期走勢與企業每股盈餘（EPS）高度連動。高盛預估，標普500企業EPS將
於2026年達375美元，並在2027年進一步升至415美元，對應年增率約36%與11%。在利率維持高檔、金融條件偏緊的環境
下，企業獲利仍持續處於成長軌道，將為股市提供穩健的中期上行動能。
• 本益比回落提供潛在布局契機：根據FactSet數據，標普500企業第三季整體獲利預計成長30.6%。其中，資訊科技類股獲利
成長預估高達66.5%，領先能源以外的其他產業；同時，該類股預估本益比已由今年高點回落，在獲利上修與估值修正同步
發生的背景下，浮現具吸引力的中期布局機會。
• 小結：短期市場仍可能受地緣政治與利率預期擾動，但中期趨勢仍由企業獲利擴張所主導。建議投資人採取分批布局科技股

策略，聚焦具備AI受益結構、資本支出能見度高及現金流穩健的龍頭企業。

前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。

Update
Unimicron  | Asia Pacific
Key ABF substrate customer
announces capacity reservation
agreements
What's new? According to  a press release on October 21, at GTC Berlin NVIDIA
(covered by Joseph Moore) named Unimicron as one of  three partners in its plan to
lift substrate capacity 2.5x over three years. A capacity reservation agreement
supports Unimicron’s KF (Kuangfu) plant expansion in Taiwan, with additions
expected from 2Q27 and full capacity by mid-2028.

Our read-through: We believe this implies robust multi-year, AI-driven substrate
demand. NVIDIA’s capacity reservation likely covers Unimicron's 2Q27 KF Plant 2
step flagged in July (see our earnings call takeaways), which could lower the
financial risk of overcapacity as Unimicron deploys its NT$53.7bn 2026 capex
budget. Stay OW with a price target of NT$1,775.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
Unimicron (3037.TW)
Base case, residual income (RI) valuation model, which we think derives the most accurate
value of the firm given that it takes into account cost of equity. We use a cost of equity of 9.2%
[risk-free rate of 1% (10-year Taiwan government note yield), equity risk premium of 8.7%, and
a beta of 1.0], a medium-term growth rate of 14%, and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Risks to Upside
n Better-than-expected ABF substrate demand from PC and server customers

n Capex cuts; halt to its capacity expansion plan
n Continued yield issues of alternative technology that doesn't require substrate (e.g.,
CoWoP)
Risks to Downside
n Sudden demand shortfall
n Technological change that would not require ABF substrates
n Intensifying competition
n Yield issues or production hiccups when ramping new capacity

ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

OSAT 展望樂觀，SOIC 機台具技術優勢 
 2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT:2026 年弘塑產能擴充一倍 ，1Q26 已
經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ， 目前外包比例33%，
未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外包產能受
限，同業也在搶外包產能。2027 主要成長動能仍為 OSAT，今年晶
圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年 OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，
SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026 差不多略增，2028
才會明顯大增，主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度。 
 2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量會很明顯放大:PLP 及 CoPoS 設備機
台單價很高，初估有機會是 CoWoS 翻倍，PLP 進度比較快，PLP
規格弘塑 310*310、510*515、600*600 都有，再大到 700 以上就不
做了，晶圓代工客戶規格是 310*310 但目前進度比較慢，還在實驗
線階段。力成 510*515 進度最快，4Q26 會拉第一批機台，2027 隨
力成購置的友達廠進度持續拉機台，2028-2029 年 PLP 機台出貨量
會很明顯放大。 
 維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元：基本面無變化，預估數字也沒做調
整，故維持買進評等 ，預估 2026 年 EPS 64.81 元，2027 年 EPS 93.97
元，目標價 3000 元。(2027 PER 30x)

個股報告

弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
 弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商 
弘塑為本土封測設備龍頭廠商，主要利基產品為半導體後段濕製程設備，並延
伸相關化學品、檢測設備代理及軟體解決方案。1H26 產品結構可區分為機台檢
測產品(65%)、化學產品(20%)、代理設備(15%)。2013 年合併化學品公司添鴻
(CLC)、2018 年底合併通路商佳霖(ClC)、併購資訊系統廠太引(Tyne)，投資美
國 X-Ray 檢測公司 SVXR。 客戶為國內半導體 、 光電 、記憶體及面板產業大廠，
包括台積電、矽品、日月光、力成、星科金朋、精材、聯電、茂矽、采鈺、穏
懋、中芯、宏達等。

2Q26 毛 利率 39.37%， 低於預
期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
 2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，低於預期，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元 
弘塑 2Q26 EPS 11.18 元，低於元富預估的 12.34 元，獲利達成率 91%。本業
來看，2Q26 營收 17.27 億元，+8%QOQ，+6%YOY，2Q26 毛利率 39.37%，
較 1Q26 回升 5.1 個百分點，然仍低於預估的 41%，主要是外包成本及急單增
加影響部分毛利率，預期 3Q26 毛利率持續向上改善，2Q26 EPS 11.18 元。

弘塑 3Q26 營收 22.67 億元，+31.3%QOQ，+51.8%YOY，營收達成率 98%，
略低於預期，預估 3Q26 毛利率 41%，預估 3Q26 EPS 16.47 元。

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀

營運逐季成長，OSA T展望樂觀 
2026 年弘塑擴產一倍 ，1Q26 已經完成擴建 ，3Q26 新產能已經滿載。 外包部分 ，
目前外包比例 33%，未來佔比會下降，目前外包月產能一個月 5-6 台，因為外
包產能受限 ， 同業也在搶外包產能 。2027 年擴產再 50-100%， 以租用廠房為主。
2027 主要成長動能還是 OSAT，今年晶圓代工客戶營收高於 OSAT，2027 年
OSAT 營收超過晶圓代工客戶，SOIC 機台去年小量，今年量增，2027 跟 2026
差不多略增 ，2028 才會明顯大增 ， 主要還是跟隨晶圓代工客戶 SOIC 擴廠進度，
SOIC 機台種類 6-7 種。

PLP 及 CoPoS 設備機台單價很高，是 CoWoS 翻倍，PLP 進度比較快，PLP
規格弘塑 310*310、510*515、600*600 都有，再大到 700 以上就不做了，晶圓
代工客戶 規格是 310*310 但目前進度比較慢，還在實驗線機台階段。力成
510*515 進度最快，4Q26 會拉第一批機台，2027 隨力成購置的友達廠進度持
續拉機台，2028-2029 年 PLP 量會很明顯放大。

CPO 部分，確定會出貨的是 EIC 跟 PIC 兩個晶片 Bonding 在一起的設備，跟
SOIC 類似，PIC 的機台目前進度很慢，因為 35 族會有污染問題，Sillicon 平面
長線做濕蝕刻這塊不做，會產生治癌物。

維持買進，目標價 3000 元

維持買進評等，目標價 3000 元 
基本面無變化，預估數字也沒做調整，OSAT 拉貨樂觀，長線 SOIC 機台放量可
期，故維持買進評等，預估 2026 年 EPS 64.81 元，2027 年 EPS 93.97 元，目
標價 3000 元。(2027 PER 30x)

風險提示 
出貨不如預期，ASP 不如預期。

## Document 209: [329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가드디어 엔진 증설, 저평_dc1151a4

Source PDF: all report pdf/[329180] HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가HD현대중공업(329180) 드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가드디어 엔진 증설, 저평_dc1151a4.pdf

2026.09.11  
HD 현대중공업 (329180)

드디어 엔진 증설, 저평가된 주가  Flash Note 
[조선/기계/방산] 변용진 2122-9181 yongjin.byun@imfnsec.com

Check Point  Buy (Maintain) 
HD현대중공업 중형엔진 및 SMR 공장 증설 결정  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건

목표주가(12M) 860,000 원(유지) 
종가(2026.09.10) 453,500 원 
상승여력 89.6 %

HD현대중공업, 중형엔진 및 SMR 시설투자 공시  
기다리던 엔진 증설의 세부 사항이 드디어 공개되었다. HD 현대중공업은 10일 공시를 통해 총 1조 722억원(중형엔진 
8,336억원/SMR 2,386억원) 규모의 시설투자 계획을 밝혔다. 먼저 중형엔진 관련, 글로벌 전력 수요 증가에 따른 엔진발전 시장 
확대에 대응 목적으로 생산능력 확충을 위한 토지 취득 및 공장 신설 및 생산설비 구축에 28년 5월까지 투자가 진행될 예정이다. 
중형엔진의 생산 능력은 현재 연간 3GW급에서 증설이 완료되면 7.2GW급으로 확장되며, 첫 엔진 출하는 28년 하반기로 예상된다. 
증설되는 신공장의 가동률은 28년 30%로 출발하여 30년 100%로 올라설 전망이다. 이에 따라 연간 1GW당 약 1조원의 매출과 
20%이상의 영업이익(현 엔진사업부 이상) 수준으로 추정되며, 계획대로 증설이 완료되고 일감을 확보한다면 총 4조원 이상의 매출 
증대 효과가 기대된다. SMR 투자 관련해서는 SMR 주기기 제작사업 진입 및 수주물량 증가에 대응하기 위해 SMR 전용 공장, 설비 
구축 및 기존 공장 최적화에 29년 4월까지 2,386억원이 투입된다. 이는 연간으로 Terrapower의 Natrium SMR 주기기 2기(각 
345MW)를 제작할 수 있는 규모다. HD현대중공업은 이번 증설이 데이터센터용 엔진만을 위한 것이 아님을 강조했다. 데이터센터 뿐 
아니라 국가전력망, 원전용 비상발전기 등 전세계적으로 증가하는 육상용 발전엔진 수요에 대응하기 위함이며, 최근 미국의 육상 
데이터센터 사업이 님비현상 등으로 일부 좌초되고 있음에도 그보다 더 큰 그림에 베팅한 것이다.  
엔진도 끌어올리지 못하는 조선업 주가, 그래도 펀더멘털은 굳건  
분명한 기업가치 증분의 근거가 되는 증설공시였음에도 불구하고 HD현대중공업의 주가는 발표 이후 하락으로 마감하였다. 올해 
조선업 주가는 YTD 평균 10%이상 하락하며 약세를 보이고 있는 상태다. (HD현대중공업 -10.0%, 삼성중공업 -11.6%, 한화오션 -
25.5%)  
조선사 공히 2분기의 실적 서프라이즈와 함께 올해 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하는 등 우수한 펀더멘털을 유지하고 있으나 이는 
주가 상승재료로 반영되고 있지 않다. 7~8월 반도체발 주식시장 혼란에 따른 전체적 수급 약세도 무시할 수 없는 요인이다. 여기에 
지난해 주가 상승의 주요 동력으로 작용하였던 미국 함정 사업 관련 협력 진전이 느린 점 등이 주가 약세의 주요 원인으로 
분석된다. 백악관 및 美해군에서는 지속적으로 함정 해외 건조의 필요성을 역설하고 있으나 美입법부에서는 지역구 의원의 반대 
등으로 관련 법안의 입법 동력이 약해져 있는 상황이다. 美국회의 이번 회기(119대 회기, ~2027년 1월 3일까지)가 이미 막바지에 
다다르고 있으며 11월 중간선거 일정까지 감안하면 실질적으로 이번 회기중에 관련 법안이 통과 및 발효될 가능성은 미미하기 
때문에, 2026년 남은 하반기에 함정사업 관련 조선업 주가 동력을 기대하기는 힘들어졌다. 다가오고 있는 3분기 실적도 원화 강세 
및 여름휴가/사고 등에 따른 조업일수 하락, 후판 유통가 상승 등 감익 요인이 산적해 있어 2분기에 비해 쉽지 않은 상황이다.  
그러나 여전히 본업인 조선업의 체력은 훌륭하다. 전세계 선박 발주량은 8월 누적기준 5,972만 CGT로 전년동기 대비 +60.6% 많은 
발주가 나오고 있으며 이는 2021년 이후 가장 강한 발주세다. 한국 조선사의 주력 선종인 LNG, 탱커, 컨테이너선의 발주 비중이 
전체 발주량의 67%를 차지하고 있으며 이에 힘입어 조선사들은 수주목표를 사실상 초과 달성하였다. (상선기준, HD현대중공업 달성 
130.6억 달러/목표 114.7억, 삼성중공업 달성 61억/목표 57억, 한화오션 달성 60억/목표없음) 3분기 실적은 다소 쉬어갈 수 
있으나 여전히 수주잔고에 기반한 추정상 매출/영업이익은 2028년까지 꾸준한 상승이 예상된다. 작년보다 오히려 하락한 주가가 
현저한 저평가일 수밖에 없는 이유다. 조선업 비중확대 및 HD현대중공업 Top Pick 의견을 유지한다.

 당 보고서에 게재된 내용들은 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하고 있으며, 외부의 부당한 압력이나 간섭 없이 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
본 분석자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위한 참고자료이며, 따라서, 본 자료에 의한 투자자의 투자결과에 대해 어떠한 목적의 증빙자료로도 사용될 수 없으며, 어떠한 경우에도 작성자 및 
당사의 허가 없이 전재, 복사 또는 대여될 수 없습니다. 무단전재 등으로 인한 분쟁발생시 법적 책임이 있음을 주지하시기 바랍니다.

[Nhận định xu hướng] 
VN-Index có thể hồi kỹ thuật từ cạnh dưới tam giác, song việc chớm thủng hỗ trợ 
1.710 điểm và số mã còn giữ xu hướng tăng từ đáy tháng 7 suy gi ảm khiến xu 
hướng ngắn hạn trở nên trung tính.

### Page 2

2

Điểm nhấn thị trường 
Chỉ số, nhóm ngành, khối ngoại và phái sinh

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -29.18 điểm tương ứng -1.68%, với thanh khoản suy 
giảm. Dầu khí, Bảo hiểm và Dịch vụ phân phối là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất 
trong tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -3,013 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở các mã MCH, VIC và FPT; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 
887 tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại đảo chiều bán ròng -2,021 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần 
qua và đang duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế -4,384 hợp đồng. Tự doanh đảo chiều bán ròng -
4,275 hợp đồng và đang duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -7,815 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ tư liên tiếp, khi VN-Index lùi về đóng cửa 1.706 
điểm - giảm khoảng 1,7% so với tuần trước và đang dao động quanh vùng hỗ trợ tâm lý 
1.700 điểm, cũng là vùng đáy cũ của tháng 8. Thanh khoản thị trường tiếp tục suy yếu, 
phản ánh tâm lý thận trọng cao độ trong bối cảnh thị trường thiếu vắng thông tin hỗ trợ 
trong nước, trong khi các yếu tố bên ngoài như xung đột tại Iran, và chi phí vốn toàn cầu 
chưa hạ nhiệt tiếp tục đè nặng tâm lý nhà đầu tư. Khối ngoại duy trì chuỗi bán ròng kéo 
dài nhiều phiên liên tiếp, nối dài đà rút vốn đã lên tới xấp xỉ 95 nghìn tỷ đồng từ đầu năm. 
Trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo kết quả kinh doanh quý 3 được kỳ vọng là chất xúc 
tác giúp thị trường giao dịch cân bằng trở lại.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX tiếp tục hình thành mẫu nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên đồ thị tuần, đánh dấu tuần 
giảm thứ 4 liên tiếp, cho thấy áp lực phân phối vẫn đang có phần lấn át. Trên khung ngày, 
tuy chỉ số đã có nỗ lực phục hồi trở lại trong 3 phiên đan xen, đặc biệt là phản ứng trong 
phiên cuối tuần khi chạm trendline trung hạn, nhưng việc chỉ có thể hình thành các mẫu 
nến "spinning" hàm ý đà tích lũy chưa thực sự thuyết phục. Trong một vài phiên kế tiếp, 
VNINDEX có thể xuất hiện nhịp hồi kỹ thuật từ vùng hỗ trợ, tương ứng với cạnh dưới tam 
giác, nhưng sau khi chớm phá vùng hỗ trợ quanh 1710 và hiện chỉ còn một số ít các mã giữ 
được xu hướng tăng tính từ đáy tháng 7, xu hướng ngắn hạn đang trở nên khá trung tính.

(Ghi chú: NĐT có thể dựa theo nhận định xu hướng thị trường để tham khảo khuyến nghị 
hành động tương ứng trong bảng phía trên) 
Kháng cự xa: 1780 (+-15) 
Kháng cự gần: 1740 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ gần: 1680 (+-10) 
Hỗ trợ xa: 1630 (+-15)

2 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
MACRO STRATEGY
The U.S. Capex Boom No One is Talking About—And Its Implications
Joseph Quinlan, Managing Director and Head of Market Strategy
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
We have long been of the belief that the U.S. is on the cusp of an inbound FDI capex boom
that can result in numerous positive spillover effects for the U.S. economy. The latest

figures from the U.S. Department of Commerce bear this out.
Exhibit 1 highlights the growing push among foreign multinationals to be inside the U.S.,
with FDI totaling nearly $240B in the first six months of this year. That is a staggering
103% jump from a year ago and places FDI inflows on track for a record year.
The latest inbound capex already adds to the massive scale of foreign investment in the
U.S. To wit, the accumulated stock of FDI in the U.S. stands at nearly $6T, based on the

latest figures from the Bureau of Economic Analysis (BEA). Manufacturing accounts for
roughly 43% of the total, and, at last count, some 10,000 foreign affiliates were operating
in the U.S.
These firms are largely invisible to the average U.S. investor, but the activities of U.S.
affiliates of foreign multinationals are substantial and competitive- and growth-enhancing.
To this point and based on the latest data from the BEA, foreign affiliates in the U.S.

employed nearly 8.6 million U.S. workers in 2024. That represents 6.1% of total private
sector employment, with manufacturing and retail the two most prominent sectors
of employment. Roughly 24% of all manufacturing jobs in the U.S. are from foreign
subsidiaries. In terms of pay, U.S. workers at these firms make $89,000 annually, 7% higher
than the national average, according to the Global Business Alliance.
Foreign affiliates also contributed some $1.5T to U.S. GDP; comprised some 15.7% of total

U.S. private sector investment; accounted for 12.4% of total U.S. research & development
(R&D) expenditures; paid roughly 20% of all U.S. federal taxes; and made up 22.5% of
U.S. exports and 26.2% of U.S. imports. 1 These are the direct effects of U.S. subsidiaries
of foreign companies operating in the U.S. The indirect effects are even greater in terms
of more income, jobs, growth and tax revenue—all bolstering the long-term competitive
advantage of the U.S. In a nutshell, foreign firms operating in the U.S. are an important

ingredient of U.S. economic growth.
There are multiple factors behind surging inflows: One, due to rising U.S. trade
protectionism, foreign multinationals increasingly want to be “inside” the U.S. economy,
not outside. Companies that once served the U.S. market exclusively through exports
increasingly face higher costs and more uncertainties and hence want to be “inside.”
Two, with a $32T-plus economy with extraordinary consumer purchasing power, foreign

firms prefer to be closer to their customers via investment than just trade (exports). There
is no more dynamic consumer in the world than the American shopper.
Three, America’s massive investment cycle in AI, data centers, semiconductors, power
generation, etc., has created numerous investment opportunities for many foreign firms
that don’t want to miss out on America’s surging capex cycle.
Four, and finally, abundant energy supplies, deep capital markets and a highly developed

technological ecosystem have also induced more FDI inflows.
As more U.S. affiliates of foreign multinationals set up shop in the U.S., think more job
creation across the country, more manufacturing output, more locally sourced purchases,
more U.S. exports, more spending on R&D, and more tax revenue for local communities.
And over the long run, think a more globally competitive U.S. economy.
1 Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign Multinational Enterprises, 2024” and “Activities of U.S. Affiliates of Foreign

Multinational Enterprises, 2023”, BEA July 2026.
Investment Implications
The boom in foreign investment in
the U.S. should continue to support
employment, productivity, corporate
earnings, and broader U.S. economic
competitiveness, in our view. Hence
our U.S. bias.

### Page 3

3 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
And speaking of global competitiveness, Exhibit 2A is very illustrative of the magnet pull of
the U.S. economy to attract FDI versus the rest of the world. Of new greenfield investment
this decade, note the overriding preference for the U.S., accounting for nearly 16% of total
global greenfield investment this decade. America’s share is larger than the next three
countries combined—the United Kingdom, India, and Germany. And note that America’s

share of new investment is more than 5X that of China’s. Most of this new investment
has gone into manufacturing sectors like electrical equipment, computers, appliances and
batteries and has originated from Asia and Europe.
The bulk of foreign investment comes from Europe, followed by Japan and Canada, but as
Exhibit 2B highlights, there is a new dynamic playing out when it comes to America’s ability
to attract capital. There is a new source in town: a handful of developing nations (namely

Taiwan, South Korea, Mexico and Brazil). Mexican manufacturers, Taiwan technology
leaders, South Korea chip leaders, Brazil industrial firms, India pharmaceutical firms—these
companies and more are realizing that in today’s trade environment, the best way to serve
the American market is not necessarily through exports but through investment—investing
locally. FDI flows from developing nations reached $63B in the 12 months through June,
more than double the level a year prior ($24.5B), according to the BEA. Fueled by insatiable

AI demand, flows from South Korea alone totaled $23.4B in the first half of 2026. The
upshot is that an increasingly important source of capital for the U.S. is coming from an
unlikely source: the developing nations.
More fire for the capex boom. The coming boom in FDI inflows is supportive of our thesis
that the U.S. stands on the cusp of a major industrial upgrade. We are in the early innings
of a structural—not cyclical—capex cycle that should lead to the rebuilding of domestic

supply chains, higher value-added manufacturing, and more AI-driven productivity growth.
Foreign firms, in our opinion, will be part of this process.
That is important to recognize because remember the following: when a foreign company
builds a factory or an R&D facility or expands its existing manufacturing capacity right
here in the U.S., good things happen. Demand for U.S. goods and services goes up (as does
demand for construction, materials, logistics and professional services). New jobs are created,

boosting incomes of U.S. workers, while supporting more local spending and consumption.
And because many foreign firms often bring advanced manufacturing technologies when
investing in the U.S., the end results include a boost in productivity and innovation in various
sectors of the economy.
All the above, of course, are the secret ingredients to stronger U.S. economic growth, rising
corporate earnings and higher equity prices. With a little help from our friends, America’s

industrial renewal is gaining strength and momentum.

MARKET VIEW
Europe’s Electoral Cycle and Market Risks
Ehiwario Efeyini, Director and Senior Investment Strategist
As midterm elections approach for the U.S. next month, voters across Europe will also
enter their own election season starting this November and continuing throughout
2027. Over the next year, 52% of the total EU population, representing 46% of EU GDP
across eight countries, will go to the polls, including voters in three of its four largest

economies (Exhibit 3). Having announced snap general elections earlier this month,
Spain will vote for members of its lower and upper house on November 29. A series of
parliamentary elections are then due for 2027 in Estonia (by March 7), Finland (on April
18), Greece (by July 25), Slovakia (by September 28), Poland (by November 11) and Italy
(by December 22). And perhaps most consequentially France will stage Presidential
elections on April 18, with a potential second round of voting set for May 2.

For the EU as a whole, the significance of these coming elections will be the potential
for a shift toward more populist, domestically oriented policy priorities from leading
opposition candidates (particularly in France, Spain and Poland) which could obstruct the
process of closer EU integration. In the nearer term, adoption of the new seven-year
(2028-2034) EU budget due by the end of 2027 could be impeded. And over the longer
term, renewed efforts toward an EU-wide capital market union to deepen and attract

more inward investment into European markets could also be further delayed.
At the country level, investors have seen bond yields rise globally in 2026 on the back of
higher inflation and concerns over fiscal sustainability. The debt question has arguably
been most pressing in parts of Europe, especially given the likelihood that upcoming
elections make for domestic increases in government largesse. And of the eight
countries going to the polls over the next year, France remains the biggest source of

concern. Interest rate spreads on French 10-year sovereign bonds have overtaken those
in both Greece and Italy this year (Exhibit 4), driven by investor concerns over public
debt. The 2027 government budget unveiled earlier this month projected a deficit of
5.0% of GDP (well in excess of the 3% specified in the EU Stability and Growth Pact),
but remained insufficiently expansive to nullify the risk that the current government
could be unseated later this year if it is forced through the fragmented parliament. This

comes on top of an existing debt load of close to 120% of GDP and a halving of official
2026 growth forecasts from 1.0% at the start of the year to 0.5% in September. In
short, all four variables in France’s debt sustainability equation currently stand as major
headwinds, and the two leading candidates in the 2027 election have shown limited
appetite for fiscal consolidation over the coming years.
Exhibit 3: Europe's Upcoming Elections Represent Around Half of EU GDP and Population.

Investment Implications
We remain cautious on European
markets overall, despite seeing
pockets of strength in selected
areas such as defense, data center
infrastructure and renewable energy.
On top of growing uncertainty
from the upcoming electoral cycle

and interest rate outlook, rising
competition and slowing demand
from China continue to challenge
key segments of the Consumer
Discretionary sector. And limited
direct AI concentration also leaves
Europe under-exposed to the key
growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and
emerging Asia.

Intervention from the ECB under its 2022 bond purchase program is also unlikely at this

stage given that the spread widening in France has not appeared either disorderly or
unwarranted. Meanwhile, conditions could potentially be aggravated by expected ECB rate
hikes in response to still above-target inflation across the eurozone. Indeed recent moves
to tighten monetary policy from a number of major central banks have been read most
hawkishly in Europe. Most of the increase in European bond yields has come from higher
inflation breakevens as opposed to real yields in the U.S. and Japan. And despite some

paring back of tightening expectations in the wake of recent bond volatility, the market is
still pricing three more policy rate increases over the next 12 months.
Euro depreciation and an emerging shift toward more protectionist trade policy with China
could further complicate the ECB policy outlook. China posted a record $35B trade surplus,
according to Eurostat, with the EU in September (alongside its $938B total surplus for the
first three quarters of 2026), and the two markets have agreed to reduce the EU bilateral

deficit by capping imports of hybrid cars for four years. Hybrids account for less than 2%
of total EU imports from China, but this could potentially mark the start of a broader shift
toward more trade protection in Europe with its largest foreign supplier which risks adding
further upward pressure to cost-push inflation. And though the leading candidates in the
French election may favor resuming oil and gas imports from Russia, Europe also remains

among the most at risk from the global energy shock. Indeed some 56% of the region’s
crude oil consumption is in the form of diesel product according to the International Energy
Agency, more than twice the U.S. share.
The 2- to 10-year eurozone yield curve has already flattened significantly since the start
of the year. And at just over 30 basis points as of mid-October, it remains flatter than
its equivalents in the U.S., UK and Japan. Outside Energy and Technology, Financials have

been among the strongest-performing sectors in Europe this year. But additional ECB
rate hikes could lead to further curve flattening, compressing bank net interest margins
and contributing to slower growth—particularly given that rate increases are coming in
response to supply-driven inflation rather than reflecting stronger economic activity.
We nonetheless continue to see pockets of strength in European markets. Growing
defense budgets should remain a tailwind for Industrials as national governments look to

meet their North Atlantic Treaty Organization targets over the next several years. And the
sector should be further supported by indirect exposure to AI investment through electric
cabling, power infrastructure and building materials for data centers. Leaders in renewable
energy equipment and services are also likely to be beneficiaries of grid upgrades and
broadening electrification globally, particularly given elevated fossil fuel prices. But our view

on the region remains cautious overall. On top of growing uncertainty from the upcoming
electoral cycle and interest rate outlook, rising competition and slowing demand from
China continue to challenge key segments of Europe’s Consumer Discretionary sector.
Limited direct AI concentration in technology hardware, cloud services and application
software also leaves Europe underexposed to the key growth theme that continues to buoy
equity markets in the U.S., Japan and emerging Asia.

### Page 6

6 of 8 October 19, 2026 – Capital Market Outlook RETURN TO FIRST PAGE
THOUGHT OF THE WEEK
Cautious Optimism Entering Fiscal Year 2027
Ariana Chiu, Assistant Vice President and Investment Strategist
“A renewed focus on rebuilding fiscal space is urgent, given elevated debt, higher
borrowing costs, multiplying demands on public budgets and heightened uncertainty.” So
warned the IMF about the global fiscal outlook last week in its updated World Economic

Outlook report. Much is at stake for developed nations in Europe and Asia facing high
existing debt, unfavorable demographics and more modest growth.
For the U.S., between structurally higher mandatory outlays, the administration’s $1.5T
proposed defense package, and the 10-year U.S. Treasury yield sitting above 5%, spending
is likely headed higher. We continue to fade concerns of an impending U.S. debt crisis,
however, largely because economic growth continues to surprise to the upside. Expanding

output is good news for the debt-to-GDP ratio, which remains a touch below 100%.
Indeed, rather than being led higher on account of un-anchored inflation expectations, the
10-year U.S. Treasury yield has repriced to reflect better-than-expected nominal growth
(Exhibit 5A). Higher yields in the face of slowing growth, meanwhile, would be a different
story, not only for the fiscal outlook (worse) but also for U.S. equity valuations (lower).
Of course, after the U.S. accumulated another $2T deficit in FY 2026, 2 “rebuilding fiscal

space” in the IMF’s words will be politically unpopular and mathematically challenging.
In our view, the biggest near-term tail risk would be a surprise move lower in growth as
budget deficits at 6% of GDP in good times leave little room for bad times. This isn’t our
base case given robust consumer spending per our internal credit and debit card data and
ongoing investment in the AI buildout and beyond. We expect these two pillars to sustain
growth in 2027.

The other good news is that, contrary to concerns, demand for Treasurys remains robust.
Yields are attractive—that was the message from October’s successful $39B 10-year
Treasury note auction. At the same time, U.S. debt boasts an array of buyers: domestic
households, the Federal Reserve, mutual funds and exchange-traded funds, money market
funds, pension funds, foreign central banks, private investors overseas, and more. Per
Exhibit 5B, whereas foreign official investors once dominated some 40% of the U.S.

Treasury market during the 2008/2009 Great Financial Crisis, their share has fallen to
12% while domestic private investors now account for a majority of holdings. Translated:
demand is more diverse than most appreciate.
Exhibit 5: Higher Yields Meet Shifting Composition of U.S. Treasury Demand.

yields present a compelling entry point
for investors seeking income and can

the U.S. over developed economies in

fiscal instability.

Index Definitions
Securities indexes assume reinvestment of all distributions and interest payments. Indexes are unmanaged and do not take into account fees or expenses. It is not possible to
invest directly in an index. Indexes are all based in U.S. dollars.
S&P 500 Index is a stock market index tracking the stock performance of 500 leading companies listed on stock exchanges in the United States.

Bank of America, Merrill, their affiliates and advisors do not provide legal, tax or accounting advice. Clients should consult their legal and/or tax advisors before making any financial decisions.
This material does not take into account a client’s particular investment objectives, financial situations, or needs and is not intended as a recommendation, offer, or solicitation for the purchase
or sale of any security or investment strategy. Merrill offers a broad range of brokerage, investment advisory and other services. There are important differences between brokerage and

of America, Merrill or any affiliate to sell or provide, or a specific invitation for a consumer to apply for, any particular retail financial product or service that may be available.
The Chief Investment Office (“CIO”) provides thought leadership on wealth management, investment strategy and global markets; portfolio management solutions; due diligence; and solutions
oversight and data analytics. CIO viewpoints are developed for Bank of America Private Bank, a division of Bank of America, N.A., (“Bank of America”) and Merrill Lynch, Pierce, Fenner & Smith

The Global Wealth & Investment Management Investment Strategy Committee (“GWIM ISC”) is responsible for developing and coordinating recommendations for short-term and long-term
investment strategy and market views encompassing markets, economic indicators, asset classes and other market-related projections affecting GWIM.
BofA Global Research is research produced by BofA Securities, Inc. (“BofAS”) and/or one or more of its affiliates. BofAS is a registered broker-dealer, Member SIPC and wholly owned subsidiary

of Bank of America Corporation.
All recommendations must be considered in the context of an individual investor’s goals, time horizon, liquidity needs and risk tolerance. Not all recommendations will be in the best interest
of all investors.
Asset allocation, diversification and rebalancing do not ensure a profit or protect against loss in declining markets.
Investments have varying degrees of risk. Some of the risks involved with equity securities include the possibility that the value of the stocks may fluctuate in response to events specific to

the companies or markets, as well as economic, political or social events in the U.S. or abroad. Small cap and mid cap companies pose special risks, including possible illiquidity and greater price
volatility than funds consisting of larger, more established companies. Investing in fixed-income securities may involve certain risks, including the credit quality of individual issuers, possible
prepayments, market or economic developments and yields and share price fluctuations due to changes in interest rates. When interest rates go up, bond prices typically drop, and vice versa.

Investments in high-yield bonds (sometimes referred to as “junk bonds”) offer the potential for high current income and attractive total return, but involves certain risks. Changes in economic
conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect a junk bond issuer’s ability to make principal and interest payments. Income from investing in municipal bonds is generally exempt
from Federal and state taxes for residents of the issuing state. While the interest income is tax-exempt, any capital gains distributed are taxable to the investor. Income for some investors

may be subject to the Federal Alternative Minimum Tax (AMT). Treasury bills are less volatile than longer-term fixed income securities and are guaranteed as to timely payment of principal and
interest by the U.S. government. Bonds are subject to interest rate, inflation and credit risks. Investments in foreign securities (including ADRs) involve special risks, including foreign currency
risk and the possibility of substantial volatility due to adverse political, economic or other developments. These risks are magnified for investments made in emerging markets. Investments in

a certain industry or sector may pose additional risk due to lack of diversification and sector concentration. There are special risks associated with an investment in commodities, such as gold,
including market price fluctuations, regulatory changes, interest rate changes, credit risk, economic changes and the impact of adverse political or financial factors.
Alternative investments are speculative and involve a high degree of risk.
Alternative investments are intended for qualified investors only. Alternative Investments such as derivatives, hedge funds, private credit, private equity funds, and funds of funds can result

in higher return potential but also higher loss potential. Changes in economic conditions or other circumstances may adversely affect your investments. Before you invest in alternative
investments, you should consider your overall financial situation, how much money you have to invest, your need for liquidity, and your tolerance for risk.

2Q2026

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu

Mở rộng mạng lưới tạo động lực tăng 
trưởng doanh thu mới

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ cải thiện biên lợi nhuận

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá mục 
tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), với 
động lực chủ yếu đến từ (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn 
Nhất và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ VND 
(+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Chúng tôi dự phóng lượng khách quốc tế/nội địa tại các thị trường AST khai thác 
tăng bình quân 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030. Lưu lượng và tỷ trọng khách quốc tế 
gia tăng được kỳ vọng thúc đẩy số giao dịch và doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

Chúng tôi dự phóng số điểm kinh doanh AST tăng từ 145 điểm cuối năm 2026 lên 
182 điểm năm 2030. Sau đóng góp từ 19 điểm tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất và 6 điểm mới tại 
T2 Nội Bài từ 2025 - 2026, các dự án Long Thành, Phú Quốc và Gia Bình tiếp tục mở 
ra dư địa mở rộng mạng lưới trong trung và dài hạn cho doanh nghiệp.

Việc mở rộng các hoạt động mang lại lợi nhuận cao, bao gồm hàng miễn thuế, 
phòng chờ thương gia và tối ưu hoạt động tại các điểm kinh doanh được kỳ vọng 
tác động tích cực lên biên lợi nhuận của AST. Chúng tôi dự phóng biên lợi nhuận 
gộp đạt 64.7%/65.5% trong năm 2026/2027, hỗ trợ LNST công ty mẹ tăng lần lượt 
48%/17% yoy.

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với AST, giá mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu, tương 
đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 21/09/2026.

Cơ cấu doanh thu 2025 Hoạt động kinh doanh

CTCP Dịch vụ Hàng không Taseco là một 
trong những doanh nghiệp có quy mô mạng 
lưới điểm kinh doanh lớn trong lĩnh vực dịch 
vụ phi hàng không tại Việt Nam, với 141 
điểm kinh doanh tại 6 sân bay quốc tế tính 
đến cuối tháng 6/2026. Hoạt động cốt lõi của 
công ty bao gồm bán lẻ bách hóa lưu niệm 
(BHLN), hàng miễn thuế, dịch vụ ăn uống và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Ngoài ra, AST còn 
tham gia lĩnh vực suất ăn hàng không thông 
qua khoản đầu tư tại công ty liên kết VinaCS.

11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030.

điểm và tăng lên 182 điểm vào 2030.

2Q2026, AST ghi nhận doanh thu và LNST đạt 479/92 tỷ VND (+22%/19% yoy), 
với động lực: (1) đóng góp từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
và (2) tăng trưởng tích cực của các mảng hàng miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood 
và phòng chờ thương gia. Lũy kế 6T2026, doanh thu và LNST đạt 1,035/221 tỷ 
VND (+33%/46% yoy), lần lượt hoàn thành 48%/49% kế hoạch năm.

Biểu đồ 5. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh của AST

Nguồn:AST

Tăng trưởng khách qua cảng hàng 
không thúc đẩy doanh thu tại các 
điểm kinh doanh hiện hữu 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng tăng trưởng lưu lượng hành khách, đặc biệt là sự gia tăng tỷ 
trọng khách quốc tế, sẽ thúc đẩy doanh thu và hiệu quả kinh doanh của AST 
trong giai đoạn 2026–2030. Theo Cục Hàng không Việt Nam, lượng khách thông 
qua các cảng hàng không trong 7T2026 đạt hơn 76 triệu lượt (+7% yoy), trong 
đó khách quốc tế đạt gần 30 triệu lượt (+11% yoy), trong khi khách nội địa ghi 
nhận tăng trưởng 4% yoy. Xu hướng này được hỗ trợ bởi nhu cầu du lịch quốc 
tế, chính sách thị thực thuận lợi và việc mở rộng mạng bay, trong khi tăng 
trưởng kinh tế và thu nhập tiếp tục tạo nền tảng cho nhu cầu đi lại trong nước. 
Trên cơ sở đó, chúng tôi dự phóng lượt khách quốc tế và nội địa tại các thị 
trường AST khai thác tăng bình quân lần lượt 11% và 5%/năm đến 2030, qua đó 
nâng dần tỷ trọng khách quốc tế trong tổng lưu lượng, thúc đẩy doanh 
thu/điểm kinh doanh nhờ: (1) lưu lượng hành khách qua cảng hàng không gia 
tăng và (2) tỷ trọng khách quốc tế cao hơn hỗ trợ doanh thu bình quân trên mỗi 
khách, đặc biệt tại mảng miễn thuế, bán lẻ và phòng chờ.

 Biểu đồ 7. Doanh thu/điểm kinh doanh AST (tỷ VND)

Nguồn: AST  Nguồn: AST

Mở rộng mạng lưới tại các nhà ga 
hiện hữu và sân bay mới, gia tăng 
quy mô kinh doanh đến 2030 
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng chu kỳ mở rộng hạ tầng hàng không giai đoạn 2026–2030 sẽ 
tạo dư địa gia tăng quy mô kinh doanh cho AST, với số điểm kinh doanh dự 
phóng đạt 145 điểm vào cuối 2026 và tăng lên 182 điểm vào năm 2030. Cụ thể: 
- Trong ngắn hạn, tăng trưởng năm 2026 được hỗ trợ bởi việc ghi nhận 
đủ năm doanh thu từ 19 điểm kinh doanh mở mới tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất 
trong năm 2025, cùng 6 điểm mới tại T2 Nội Bài trong năm 2026, trong 
đó có 2 cửa hàng miễn thuế và 2 cửa hàng Memories of Vietnam khai 
trương trong 2Q2026. 
- Trong trung và dài hạn, các dự án Cảng HKQT Long Thành, Cảng HKQT 
Phú Quốc nâng cấp mở rộng và Cảng HKQT Gia Bình sẽ góp phần thúc 
đẩy ngành hàng không Việt Nam nói chung, bổ sung mặt bằng thương 
mại, mở ra cơ hội phát triển các mảng kinh doanh cốt lõi cho AST nói 
riêng bao gồm các mảng bán lẻ, miễn thuế, nhà hàng - fastfood và 
phòng chờ thương gia. Với kinh nghiệm khai thác 141 điểm kinh doanh 
tại 6 sân bay quốc tế và lịch sử trúng thầu tại T3 Tân Sơn Nhất, T2 Nội 
Bài trong thời gian qua, chúng tôi kỳ vọng AST có khả năng cạnh tranh 
cao trong việc đấu thầu quyền khai thác mặt bằng tại các sân bay mới, 
đặc biệt là Gia Bình, qua đó duy trì vị thế và thị phần kinh doanh phi 
hàng không tại khu vực phía Bắc khi một phần lưu lượng hành khách 
được phân bổ lại từ Nội Bài.

Cơ cấu kinh doanh và hiệu quả vận 
hành hỗ trợ mở rộng biên lợi nhuận  
 Chúng tôi kỳ vọng biên lợi nhuận của AST tiếp tục cải thiện trong 2026 - 2027, 
nhờ định hướng mở rộng các mảng có biên lợi nhuận tốt như hàng miễn thuế 
và phòng chờ thương gia, kết hợp tối ưu hiệu quả vận hành tại các điểm kinh 
doanh. Cụ thể: (1) AST ưu tiên gia tăng số điểm kinh doanh hàng miễn thuế 
nhằm đón đầu tăng trưởng nhanh và ổn định của lượng khách quốc tế, qua đó 
nâng đóng góp lợi nhuận từ hoạt động mua sắm tại sân bay, (2) mở rộng mạng 
lưới phòng chờ, đồng thời gia tăng lượng khách sử dụng trên nền năng lực phục 
vụ hiện hữu và (3) tối ưu hóa hiệu quả hoạt động thông qua cải thiện bố trí mặt 
bằng, danh mục sản phẩm và kiểm soát chi phí tại các điểm kinh doanh, qua 
đó tăng doanh thu và lợi nhuận thu được trên mỗi khách hàng.

Nguồn: Báo cáo công ty, KBSV

Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA, giá 
mục tiêu 76,500 VND/cổ phiếu 
 Chúng tôi khuyến nghị MUA đối với cổ phiếu AST, với giá mục tiêu 76,500 
VND/cổ phiếu, tương đương tiềm năng tăng giá 20% so với giá đóng cửa ngày 
21/09/2026, dựa trên triển vọng tăng trưởng từ lượng khách quốc tế, mở rộng 
mạng lưới điểm kinh doanh và khả năng cải thiện biên lợi nhuận của doanh 
nghiệp. Chúng tôi sử dụng phương pháp định giá từng phần (SOTP), bao gồm 
DCF cho hoạt động kinh doanh cốt lõi của AST và P/E mục tiêu 11x đối với phần 
lợi nhuận thuộc sở hữu AST tại VinaCS. Các giả định chính gồm: (1) lượng khách 
quốc tế/nội địa tăng trung bình 11%/5% mỗi năm đến 2030, (2) số điểm kinh 
doanh tăng từ 145 điểm cuối 2026 lên 182 điểm năm 2030, với giả định AST 
trúng thầu mặt bằng tại cả Long Thành và Gia Bình và (3) biên lợi nhuận gộp 
2026/2027 đạt 64.7/65.5%. Đối với VinaCS, chúng tôi kỳ vọng nhà máy suất ăn 
Long Thành sẽ hỗ trợ tăng trưởng lợi nhuận trong trung và dài hạn khi dự kiến 
có thể bắt đầu đóng góp lợi nhuận cho AST từ 2028.

Rủi ro đối với định giá bao gồm: (1) AST không giành được quyền khai thác tại 
Long Thành và Gia Bình như kỳ vọng, (2) lưu lượng khách dịch chuyển giữa các 
sân bay mới và hiện hữu khiến doanh thu tại một số điểm kinh doanh bị sụt 
giảm, (3) cạnh tranh gia tăng từ các doanh nghiệp kinh doanh dịch vụ phi hàng 
không khác và (4) căng thẳng địa chính trị kéo dài, giá dầu tăng gây áp lực lên 
hoạt động hàng không và chi tiêu của hành khách.

## Document 296: First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ household incomes, assets and savings update - June quarter 2026 (2026-10-24).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-10-24
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#consumer #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ household incomes, assets and savings
update - June quarter 2026
By Satish Ranchhod
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
10:24 October 08 2026 Share
New Zealand incomes and savings are rising, as is the value of households’ financial
assets. However, the value of housing assets remains flat.
Key takeouts from Stats NZ’s latest update on household incomes, assets and savings (June quarter
2026)

### Page 2

Household incomes
Disposable income levels in the economy grew by 5.2% in the year to June, down from 6.5% in the
year to March

Savings and wealth
Household savings have continued to rise, climbing by $2.5b over the past quarter.

Overall household wealth levels were up 1.9% over the past year. Under the surface, the make-up of
household wealth continues to shift.

Interest costs 
Overall household income levels are continuing to rise. Much of that is due to increases in
entrepreneurial earnings (which is in part due to increased earnings in the agricultural sector). Wage
and salary growth has been more limited but has picked up.
Household savings levels have been rising for three years now, with another solid rise over the past
three months. 
Overall household wealth levels have risen modestly. Although the value of households’ financial
assets is continuing to rise, housing assets have essentially shown no growth since late 2023.
Household spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter. That’s the first increase in
two years. The easing in average borrowing costs seen over the past few years has come to a close
with the RBNZ’s hiking cycle now in train.
Underpinning the rise in overall income levels have been continued increases in entrepreneurial
earnings, which are up 12% over the past year. That will include stronger earnings in the agricultural
sector, which has been boosted by the strength in commodity export prices. That lift in agricultural
earnings has been reflected in stronger economic confidence and spending in many rural regions.
We expect commodity export earnings will remain firm over the year ahead. 
In contrast, wage and salary earnings are up just 2.7% over the past year. That’s up from 1.8% in the
year to March, but it’s a fairly muted rise, especially given the continued increases in the population
and inflation of 4.1%.
June’s large increase in savings builds on the solid increases seen over the past three years.
The savings rate in the June quarter was 3.7% of disposable incomes, down just slightly from last
quarter.
The value of households’ financial assets has continued to rise and is up 4.6% over the past year. 
However, the value of housing and land assets is down 0.9% over the past year, and it has remained
essentially unchanged since late 2023. We expect housing market conditions will remain soft over
the coming year, limiting increases in overall household wealth. 
Households’ spending on interest costs rose just slightly in the June quarter, climbing by 0.3%.
While that’s a relatively small change, it is the first increase that we’ve seen in two years.

### Page 3

Related articles

Media Contact:
Satish Ranchhod, Senior Economist
P: +64 9 336 5668
M: +64 21 710 852
Over the past few years, many borrowers saw their debt servicing costs fall as they rolled off earlier
high mortgage rates and onto lower ones. That helped to support household spending.
However, the easing in average borrowing cost has now ended. Recent months have seen mortgage
rates pushing higher as the RBNZ has begun to hike the Official Cash Rate, with further hikes
expected over the coming year. 
Looking ahead, we don’t expect a significant increase in average household borrowing costs.
However, we won’t see the same support for spending that we did over the past few years.

## Document 299: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

Source PDF: all report pdf/FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb.pdf
Report source: researchmail轉寄
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

From:    Yardeni QuickTakes <yardeni-research@ghost.io>
Subject: FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus  FEMO's Steady Climb
Date:    5 hours ago
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus FEMO's Steady Climb

YARDENI QUICKTAKES
FOMO's Rollercoaster Versus
FEMO's Steady Climb
By Ed Yardeni & Elias Griepentrog • 19 Oct 2026
View in browser

### Page 2

Photo by Jonny Gios / Unsplash
I. FOMO vs FEMO
Forecasting the stock market is easy! Just multiply the P/E (price-to-
earnings ratio) by E (earnings per share) to get P (the stock price index).
The hard part, of course, is getting the P/E and E right. We call the P/E
component FOMO (fear of missing out) and the E component FEMO
(fabulous earnings momentum). This year, investors have been on a
rollercoaster ride as FOMO has fluctuated mostly to the downside, while

FEMO has continued climbing.

### Page 3

More recently, concerns about AI investment returns and rising US
Treasury yields have tightened credit conditions, raising fears about the
sustainability of the AI investment boom and weighing on P/Es. Since
early October, multiple compression has outpaced continued earnings
gains, dragging many stocks lower.
Semiconductors have been at the center of the recent rout, with the
semiconductor stock price index plunging 15.7% over eight consecutive
sessions. However, FOMO staged a comeback today, with the Nasdaq

rising 1.1% and semiconductors gaining 1.6%, snapping their losing
streak. The FOMO rollercoaster continues, but FEMO remains firmly on
an upward trajectory.
Here is more:
(1) S&P 500. So far this year, forward earnings surged 31.0% ytd through
October 19, more than offsetting a 14.2% contraction in the forward P/E.
Consequently, the index is up 12.3% ytd (chart).
(2) Information Technology. The divergence is even more striking in the
S&P 500 Information Technology sector. Forward earnings has soared

65.5% ytd, while the forward P/E has contracted 25.6%, leaving the
sector's stock price index up 23.0% ytd (chart). Since early October,
however, falling P/Es have outpaced rising earnings, pulling IT stock
prices lower.

### Page 4

(3) Semiconductors. FEMO is incredibly strong in the S&P 500
Semiconductors industry, which now accounts for a record 55.0% of the IT
sector's forward earnings and 40.9% of its market capitalization (chart).
That implies a significant forward P/E discount to the rest of the IT sector
and to the S&P 500. The discount reflects concerns that rapidly rising
borrowing costs for highly leveraged AI infrastructure providers could slow
the AI investment boom and weaken demand for chips. Semiconductor

earnings continue to soar, but that's because capacity constraints are
boosting semiconductor prices, which should fall sooner or later as either
demand slows or capacity expands, or both.
(4) Healthy constraints. We view investors' growing concerns about the
AI boom as good news. Financial, social, political, and physical
constraints should reduce overinvestment risks and keep the boom on a
sustainable path. Recently, financial constraints have been in focus, with

### Page 5

CoreWeave's bond yields surging above 15% last week as the Bond
Vigilantes increasingly scrutinized AI borrowers. The bond market is
playing a welcome role in allocating capital more efficiently. Meanwhile,
the big hyperscalers remain the boom's main drivers, supported by strong
cash flows and ample access to capital. The AI rollercoaster may be
hitting speed bumps, but that will help keep it on a steadier path.
II. Industrial Production
The strength of the US economy continues to underpin strong earnings

growth. Industrial production rose 0.7% m/m in September, its biggest
gain since April, and 2.2% y/y, the strongest since September 2022
(charts). Leading the way higher was utilities output. That reflects rising
electricity demand from data centers and manufacturing, as well as
weather-related demand. Manufacturing output edged up 0.3%.

### Page 6

Since ChatGPT was released in late 2022, technology-related industrial
production has risen sharply to record highs (chart).
Defense and space equipment production also continues to increase at a
solid pace, supported by rising military spending and the rapid growth of
the space industry (chart).

### Page 7

III. Federal Deficit
Fiscal policy also continues to boost economic growth, though at the cost
of large federal deficits and mounting federal debt. The federal budget
deficit totaled $1.99 trillion over the 12 months through September, while
marketable Treasury debt increased by $2.12 trillion (chart).
Federal outlays reached $7.40 trillion, including $3.77 trillion for health
care, Medicare, and Social Security (chart). Notably, net interest payments

reached a record $1.05 trillion, exceeding defense spending of $970
billion.

### Page 8

Tax receipts totaled $5.40 trillion, supported by rising individual income
and payroll taxes as well as tariff revenues (chart). Corporate income tax
receipts have been depressed this year by the 100% depreciation
allowance granted by last year's One Big Beautiful Bill Act (OBBB).
IV. Foreign Capital Flows
Given Corporate America's FEMO, it is not surprising that foreign capital
continues to pour into US financial markets. Over the 12 months through

August, net foreign purchases of US equities totaled a whopping $1.01
trillion, followed by corporate bonds and Treasury notes and bonds (chart).
Apparently, foreign investors have a greater appetite for US equities and
corporate bonds than for US Treasuries.

### Page 9

Net foreign equity inflows surged to a record high, far exceeding previous
peaks (chart). Private investors accounted for $844 billion, while foreign
official institutions contributed another $165 billion, a remarkable
development given their historically limited participation in US equity
markets.
Foreigners purchased more US corporate bonds than US Treasury notes
and bonds over the past 12 months (chart).

### Page 10

clause.
Yardeni QuickTakes © 2026 – Unsubscribe

## Document 303: France’s unrest adds to an already toxic economic mix

Source PDF: all report pdf/France’s unrest adds to an already toxic economic mix.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Décembre 2018 53
Conjoncture française
Impact macroéconomique du mouvement des gilets jaunes :
une estimation difficile à ce stade
Estimer l’impact économique du mouvement des
gilets jaunes est un exercice difficile et incertain à la
date de finalisation de cetteNote de conjoncture (13
décembre 2018). Ce mouvement, qui a pris une
forme assez inédite, a en effet commencé à la
mi–novembre, soit relativement récemment, et se
poursuit encore au moment où s’achève la rédaction

de cette Note. Très peu d’indicateurs
macroéconomiques sont donc connus à ce stade.
Centré au départ sur la hausse des prix du carburant
automobile, le mouvement des gilets jaunes s’est
ensuite élargi à d’autres revendications, notamment
sur le pouvoir d’achat. Il a surtout pris la forme de
blocages routiers, mais aussi de manifestations
parfois émaillées de violence, à Paris comme en
province et outre–mer.
Si ce mouvement ne comporte pas de précédent

historique directement comparable, on peut tout de
même tenter de lister les canaux par lesquels il est
susceptible d’affecter l’activité économique.
Un impact probable sur la consommation,
notamment de services
Du côté de la production industrielle, les blocages de
routes et de dépôts pétroliers ont pu engendrer des
retards de livraison et cert aines entreprises peuvent
pâtir de problèmes d’approvisionnement.
Néanmoins, ces blocages semblent à ce stade d’une

ampleur moindre que ceux qui avaient affecté
l’activité économique pendant, par exemple, les
grèves massives de décembre 1995, lesquelles
avaient ôté environ 0,2 point à la croissance
trimestrielle du PIB.
En revanche, le mouvement des gilets jaunes est de
nature à pénaliser la consommation, localement
assez durement, et par plusieurs canaux. Tout
d’abord, les blocages, notamment routiers, ont pu
conduire les ménages à reporter ou annuler certaines

dépenses. Les dépenses en biens peuvent certes être
en partie reportées. Mais cet effet de report est en
général moindre pour les dépenses en services (en
particulier dans l’hébergement–restauration, les loisirs,
les transports) qui pourraient donc connaître des pertes
sèches plus marquées.
Un effet sur le tourisme
Car, aux effets directs des blocages, s’ajoute le
caractère anxiogène des s cènes de violence qui ont
marqué le mouvement, en particulier les samedis 24

novembre et, plus encore, 1er et 8 décembre. Certaines
se sont déroulées dans des lieux symboliques du pays et
hautement touristiques (avenue des Champs–Elysées et
Arc de Triomphe à Paris). On peut donc s’attendre à ce
qu’elles pénalisent les activités liées au tourisme.
La France a déjà connu des épisodes émeutiers, par
exemple en novembre 2005. Les données disponibles
suggèrent néanmoins que ces événements n’ont sans
doute pas eu d’impact significatif sur l’activité

touristique. Celles-ci concernaient alors la région
parisienne, mais pas le cœur touristique de la capitale.
À l’inverse, les attentats de novembre 2015 à Paris,
évènements de nature certes très différente, ont pesé
pendant plusieurs mois sur le tourisme, ôtant
0,2 point de PIB à la croissance annuelle de 2016 (cf.
Insee, 2016 et Beatriz, 2018).
À titre illustratif, la chronique des glissements annuels
des nuitées dans les hôtels figure sur le graphique

ci–dessous pour la période 2015–2016. La
fréquentation touristique dans l’hôtellerie a nettement
baissé après les attentats de novembre 2015
(graphique ). C’est – logiquement – la clientèle
étrangère qui a été la plus touchée. L ’attentat du 14
juillet 2016 à Nice a sans doute eu un effet du même
type, même s’il a été moins marqué. Au moins deux
autres évènements ont vraisemblablement eu aussi un
impact sur cette série durant la période considérée : les

manifestations du printemps 2016, dont la violence
ponctuelle n’était néanmoins pas comparable à celle du
1er décembre 2018, et, à l’inverse, l’Euro de football
organisé en France en juin et juillet 2016.

### Page 2

Conjoncture française
54 Note de conjoncture
En novembre 2018, la confiance des ménages
dans la situation économique a nettement
baissé
À côté des principaux indicateurs macroéconomiques,
les enquêtes de conjoncture auprès des entreprises et
des ménages permettent d’avoir une idée de la
manière dont les acteurs économiques envisagent leur
situation à venir.
Mi–décembre, les résultats des enquêtes de
conjoncture de l’Insee auprès des entreprises ne sont

pas encore disponibles pour la période postérieure aux
manifestations émaillées de violence. Le mouvement
des gilets jaunes ayant commencé le 17 novembre, les
chefs d’entreprise n’ont pas pu les prendre en compte
dans leur réponse à l’enquête de novembre.
L ’enquête de conjoncture auprès des ménages affiche
quant à elle une nette dégradation de l’indicateur
synthétique de confiance en novembre. Toutes les
composantes de la confiance sont en baisse marquée.

En particulier, le solde sur l’opportunité de faire des
achats importants chute en novembre ; or ce solde
peut être relié à la consommation future des ménages
(cf. éclairage : Que disent les ménages sur leur
perception des évolutions de leur niveau de vie dans
les enquêtes de conjoncture ?). Il laisse donc présager
une consommation relativement peu dynamique au
quatrième trimestre 2018, trimestre pourtant marqué
par des mesures fiscales (baisse de la taxe d’habitation

et des cotisations salariales) venant augmenter le
pouvoir d’achat des ménages.
Le mouvement social pourrait également
peser sur l’emploi
Enfin, le ralentissement de l’activité lié au mouvement
des gilets jaunes pourrait également affecter la
dynamique des créations d’emploi. Par exemple,
certaines embauches de contrats courts ou d’intérim
prévus dans les magasins à l’occasion des fêtes de fin
d’année pourraient finalement ne pas se concrétiser à

cause des blocages.
Au total, le scénario de cetteNote de conjoncture fait
l’hypothèse que le mouvement des gilets jaunes
pourrait ôter 0,1 point à la croissance du PIB au
quatrième trimestre 2018, via les secteurs d’activité
sans doute principalement touchés : commerce,
hébergement, restauration, transports… Cette
estimation est néanmoins soumise à beaucoup
d’aléas, ne serait–ce que sur la durée du mouvement.
Bibliographie
Insee (2017), « En 2017, le tourisme ne pèserait plus

sur la croissance française, après avoir coûté 0,2
point en 2016 », éclairage de laNote de conjoncture
de juin 2017.
Beatriz (2018), « Le retour des touristes en 2017 a
contribué pour 0,2 point à la croissance française »,
encadré de l’édition 2018 de L’économie française,
comptes et dossiers (Insee). /c110

## Document 304: FX Daily_ Dollar has lost its safe-haven crown

Source PDF: all report pdf/FX Daily_ Dollar has lost its safe-haven crown.pdf
Report source: Unknown
Date: 未知日期
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The yen and Swiss franc have emerged as the safe havens of
choice as the greenback continues to show some vulnerability
to the AI story. A further widening in French spreads has not
materially hit the euro, but that correlation may spike again
soon. Today, PMIs are published, but focus will remain primarily
on global equities, France and oil

Frantisek Taborsky, Francesco Pesole and Chris Turner
The dollar’s vulnerability to the AI story is driving safe-haven demand
elsewhere, notably towards the yen and Swiss franc
USD: Losing safe-haven appeal
Yesterday was a rough session for risk sentiment, as global equities
sold oﬀ on a combination of higher oil prices and AI-related
concerns. FX moves conﬁrmed that the dollar is far from the
preferred safe haven at this stage, with the yen and Swiss franc
emerging as the main beneﬁciaries of the defensive rotation.
Crucially, the negative correlation between bonds and equities is
reasserting itself, and FX markets are taking their cues from that
dynamic. 10yr Treasuries broke back below 5.0% for the ﬁrst time

### Page 2

in a month, showing ever-reduced sensitivity to higher oil prices.
The same dynamic is visible at the front end, where the 2yr SOFR
swap rate dropped 15bp to 4.50% yesterday. That suggests equity
jitters are enough to dent market conviction on a large Federal
Reserve tightening cycle.
Still, the bar for pricing out a December Fed hike appears fairly high,
which should prevent larger USD corrections for the moment. Any
stabilisation in risk sentiment can help USD consolidate across

most pairs and take some shine oﬀ JPY and CHF. But the FX reaction
function to further risk sell-oﬀs is now looking much clearer.
On the data side, focus will be on S&P Global PMIs today.
Consensus is for some softening in the US ﬁgures, which, however,
remain deep into expansionary territory.
Francesco Pesole
EUR: Sensitivity to France can return
rapidly
The OAT-Bund 10yr spread closed at 174bp after touching 178bp
yesterday. That wasn’t the only EU sovereign spread widening

yesterday; bunds outperformed (+9bp) on haven demand, and
energy prices played a role too.
But OATs saw the only increase in 10yr yields in Europe (+6bp), and
the OAT-BTP spread has reached a grim 50bp milestone. The
budget/political situation is not visibly deteriorating, so the move is
probably a function of OAT’s simply having a higher beta to global
factors and perhaps some concerns about another weekend of
social unrest in France.
The euro’s sensitivity to France has clearly diminished, with the

common currency not materially underperforming G10 peers
yesterday. That link may, however, re-establish very rapidly should
a new pain level – perhaps 200bp – be breached. We aren’t
expecting that much further widening for now, but the risks are
clearly there, and the euro remains rather unattractive across the
board.

EUR/CHF remains the cleanest way to play European sovereign risk
at the moment, while EUR/USD should be quick to re-test the 1.1120
14 October low should bond turmoil persist. Today’s PMIs will
probably play a secondary role to France, which faces a Moody’s
credit review, and global equities. Expectations are for slightly softer
numbers compared to September.
Francesco Pesole
CEE: Global risk-off sentiment weighs on
region currencies
The end of the week oﬀers little in the regional calendar, with the

primary driver remaining the broader global narrative. Deepening
"risk-oﬀ" sentiment and the continued rise in energy prices are
weighing on emerging market currencies, including those in CEE.
Nevertheless, the region is demonstrating admirable resilience;
despite some ﬂuctuations in rates, the FX market remains relatively
robust. Even so, we maintain a bearish outlook on CEE currencies in
general. EUR/CZK and EUR/RON have reached the targets we
discussed here on Monday.

In the Czech Republic, we saw an interview yesterday with Czech
National Bank Deputy Governor Jan Frait, marking the start of the
current round of oﬃcial commentary ahead of the November
policy meeting. Although the market interpreted the interview as
dovish, we view it as preparation for a rate hike. Furthermore, as
Frait is one of the more dovish members of the Bank Board,
subsequent comments from other oﬃcials are likely to be more
hawkish. This supports further strengthening of the koruna, despite

a signiﬁcantly negative global environment; consequently, we
could see a test of the 24.200–24.250 range.
EUR/RON has normalised, returning to the 5.340 level, following the
parliament’s vote of conﬁdence in the new technocratic
government. As we noted previously, we do not expect the new
government to trigger an FX rally, but rather to foster stabilisation
around current levels. This remains our baseline view, though the
risk remains tilted to the upside for EUR/RON should political

uncertainty return.

US Monetary Policy FX Eurozone
Emerging markets
On the bearish side, the Polish zloty and the Hungarian forint
remain particularly vulnerable. They may face further downside,
given the absence of a hawkish stance from their respective central
banks, combined with high exposure to energy imports and rising
energy prices. The upside for EUR/HUF should be capped in the 368–
370 range, supported by a local, idiosyncratic narrative; we would
expect to see new sellers of EUR/HUF emerge there. Conversely,

EUR/PLN lacks this beneﬁt and remains exposed to the upside.
Frantisek Taborsky
MXN: Banxico has done a number on the
peso
Since US rates exploded higher at the start of September, the
Mexican peso is by far the worst-performing EM currency. It is close
to 8% weaker versus the dollar. The next worst currency is the
Romanian leu at around 4%. The peso has been hit hard by dovish
Banxico rhetoric, outlining that it did not plan to mechanically track

the Fed with rate hikes. Should Banxico fail to match a presumed
rate hike from the Fed in December, the Banxico-Fed policy rate
spread will reach the narrowest on record and leave the peso
exposed at a diﬃcult time for risk assets.
Barring a hawkish surprise from Banxico at its 5 November
meeting, we expect the peso to remain vulnerable. We note that in
the IMF ﬁnancial stability report, Mexico was seen to have the near
entirety of its foreign portfolio investments in US securities, which

the IMF saw as AI-chasing behaviour. Unrest in the tech sector may
therefore be weighing on the peso. 19.00/19.10 looks to be the
trajectory for USD/MXN over the coming weeks.
Chris Turner

## Document 315: Instacart寻求出售，股价涨超9%！DoorDash在潜在买家之列，Uber退出

Source PDF: all report pdf/Instacart寻求出售，股价涨超9%！DoorDash在潜在买家之列，Uber退出.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-23
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据媒体报道， Instacart 正在评估潜在的出售计划，已与包括 Uber 和 DoorDash 在内的潜在
买家进⾏了初步洽谈，但 Uber 已退出谈判，双⽅⽬前不再进⾏讨论。对于买家⽽⾔，收购
将进⼀步集中美国最⼤的⼏家餐饮与杂货配送平台，将⾯临严峻的反垄断审查。
摘要：
杂货配送公司 Instacart 正在评估出售选项，包括与 Uber 及 DoorDash 进⾏的初步接触，消息⼀出
Instacart 单⽇⼤涨逾 9% 。
据彭博援引知情⼈⼠透露，Instacart 近期曾主动接触 Uber 洽谈出售事宜，但 Uber 已退出谈判，
双⽅⽬前不再进⾏讨论。
据 The Information 报道，Instacart 正在评估潜在的出售计划，已与包括 Uber 和 DoorDash 在内
的潜在买家进⾏了初步洽谈，并已聘请⼀家投资银⾏评估各类选项。
消息公布后， Instacart 周五收涨 9.21% ，市值约 134 亿美元。与此同时，市场对潜在买家⾯临的
反垄断⻛险及收购成本作出反应 ——Uber 股价收跌 3.4% ， DoorDash 收跌 3.5% 。

竞争压⼒下的战略抉择
Instacart 探索出售的背后，是其在杂货配送市场⽇益承压的经营现实。
⾃ 2023 年上市以来，该公司收⼊增速持续落后于主要竞争对⼿： 2024 年及 2025 年收⼊增速均放
缓⾄约 11% ，⽽ DoorDash 去年的收⼊增速达到 28% 。
Instacart 今年第⼆季度收⼊同⽐增⻓ 14% ⾄ 10 亿美元，⾃由现⾦流达 4.80 亿美元，财务基本⾯
尚可，但在规模与增速上已明显落后于更具资源优势的竞争对⼿。
分析称，若以科技⾏业近期并购惯例的 30% ⾄ 40% 溢价计算， Instacart 的收购估值约在 160 亿⾄
170 亿美元之间。

收购将⾯临严峻的反垄断审查
对于潜在买家⽽⾔，收购 Instacart 的战略逻辑在于将其成熟的零售商合作⽹络与买家⾃⾝的配送
基础设施及⽤⼾体量相结合，以进⼀步巩固在美国杂货配送市场的地位。
值得⼀提的是， Instacart 与 Uber 之间已存在商业合作关系，⽤⼾可直接通过 Instacart 应⽤程序
订购 Uber Eats 。
然⽽，任何⼀⽅的收购都将⾯临严峻的反垄断审查。此类交易将进⼀步集中美国最⼤的⼏家餐
饮与杂货配送平台，监管层⾯的阻⼒不容忽视。
Uber 的并购⽇程同样可能形成制约。据报道，该公司今年 7 ⽉已宣布以近 150 亿美元收购德国外
卖平台 Delivery Hero ，此外还涉及对 ezCater 的收购交易，多项并购同步推进或令其在出价

Instacart 时⾯临更⼤的整合与监管压⼒。
历史谈判的前⻋之鉴
这并⾮ Instacart ⾸次⾝处并购讨论之中。据 The Information 报道， DoorDash 早在 2021 年就曾探
讨以 400 亿⾄ 500 亿美元的估值收购 Instacart ，彼时 Instacart 的私募市场估值约为 390 亿美元。
但那轮谈判最终破裂，部分原因正是双⽅对交易能否通过拜登政府的反垄断审查存有疑虑。
与 2021 年相⽐，当前市场环境已⼤相径庭 ——Instacart 估值⼤幅缩⽔⾄约 120 亿美元，但反垄断
监管的不确定性依然是悬在潜在交易上⽅的核⼼变量。各⽅谈判⽬前仍处于早期阶段，能否最
终促成交易尚⽆定论。

## Document 381: Top of Mind - Higher Bond Yields_ How Concerning_

Source PDF: all report pdf/Top of Mind - Higher Bond Yields_ How Concerning_.pdf
Report source: Goldman Sachs (Top of Mind)
Date: 2026-10-19
Pages: 31
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

The Goldman Sachs Group, Inc.
Global bond yields have risen to multi-decade highs, yet growth and markets have
remained remarkably resilient. But with some pressures beginning to emerge (e.g. in
European sovereign bond markets), can this resilience last, or will more vulnerabilities
be revealed? We ask the IMF’s Tobias Adrian, Moody’s Analytics’ Mark Zandi, and GS
economists. Adrian is relatively sanguine about the economy’s resilience, though
notes downside risks, while GS economists expect only a modest growth drag from

higher rates, but a more consequential one if yields remain near current or higher
levels. Zandi is generally more concerned, describing the US economy as vulnerable
rather than resilient, and sees one-in-three odds of the economy going off the r ails.
Meanwhile, GS strategists and analysts are not especially concerned about banks or
markets more broadly, but warn that further increases in yields or rate volatility could
test risk assets. Key to watch: whether the increasingly interconnected and leveraged

AI investment boom can continue to withstand higher rates.
Tobias Adrian, Special Advisor and former Director of Monetary and Capital Markets, IMF
“The global economy has demonstrated considerable resilience in the face of multiple severe shocks, and I would not be
surprised if it continues to shrug off higher yields, pr ovided they do not keep rising sharply in a disorderly fashion. At the same
time, I do worry about downside risks.”
Mark Zandi, Chief Economist, Moody’s Analytics
